{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011Cen9ivjgwo3WbCjLRtFQN","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01VBPbtXxfLQZEjttLjRKmED","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/llmtest/evals/20260901-162328/matmul_claude-opus-5-cc-medium_hybrid_r3/matmul/matmul.cpp","content":"#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA matrix multiplication.\n//\n// Decomposition:\n//   * MPI: the rows of A (and hence of C) are split into contiguous blocks,\n//     one block per rank. B is replicated (every rank generates it locally,\n//     the initialization is a pure function of the indices, so no\n//     communication is required).\n//   * OpenMP: matrix initialization / validation on the host, and driving all\n//     GPUs owned by a rank concurrently (one host thread per device).\n//   * CUDA: the actual DGEMM, using a register-blocked, shared-memory tiled\n//     kernel. A rank's row block is split again across the GPUs it owns.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                                     \\\n    do {                                                                                                     \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                                     \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                           \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d: %s\\n\", #call, __FILE__, __LINE__,                       \\\n                    cudaGetErrorString(err_));                                                               \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                                    \\\n        }                                                                                                    \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization\nconstexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {\n    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);\n}\n\n// Initialize the rows [rowBegin, rowEnd) of an NxN matrix into a compact buffer.\nvoid initMatrixRows(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N, const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd) {\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {\n        double* row = mat.data() + (i - rowBegin) * N;\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {\n            row[j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// --- CUDA kernel ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Tile sizes: each block computes a BM x BN tile of C, each thread a TM x TN\n// sub-tile held in registers. The per-thread elements are strided by BM/TM and\n// BN/TN so that all shared memory accesses are bank-conflict free.\nstatic constexpr int BM = 64;\nstatic constexpr int BN = 64;\nstatic constexpr int BK = 16;\nstatic constexpr int TM = 4;\nstatic constexpr int TN = 4;\nstatic constexpr int THREADS = (BM / TM) * (BN / TN); // 256\n\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS) void dgemmKernel(const double* __restrict__ A, const double* __restrict__ B,\n                                                       double* __restrict__ C, const int M, const int N) {\n    __shared__ double As[BK][BM]; // transposed A tile\n    __shared__ double Bs[BK][BN];\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const int blockRow = blockIdx.y * BM;\n    const int blockCol = blockIdx.x * BN;\n\n    // Element mapping of a thread inside the block tile (strided).\n    const int tRow = tid / (BN / TN); // 0..15\n    const int tCol = tid % (BN / TN); // 0..15\n\n    // Cooperative load mapping.\n    const int aLoadRow = tid / BK;    // 0..15, stride 16 -> covers BM = 64\n    const int aLoadCol = tid % BK;    // 0..15\n    const int bLoadRow = tid / BN;    // 0..3, stride 4 -> covers BK = 16\n    const int bLoadCol = tid % BN;    // 0..63\n\n    double acc[TM][TN];\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int m = 0; m < TM; ++m) {\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int n = 0; n < TN; ++n) {\n            acc[m][n] = 0.0;\n        }\n    }\n\n    for (int kt = 0; kt < N; kt += BK) {\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int r = 0; r < BM / (THREADS / BK); ++r) {\n            const int row = aLoadRow + r * (THREADS / BK);\n            const int gr = blockRow + row;\n            const int gc = kt + aLoadCol;\n            As[aLoadCol][row] = (gr < M && gc < N) ? A[static_cast<size_t>(gr) * N + gc] : 0.0;\n        }\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int r = 0; r < BK / (THREADS / BN); ++r) {\n            const int row = bLoadRow + r * (THREADS / BN);\n            const int gr = kt + row;\n            const int gc = blockCol + bLoadCol;\n            Bs[row][bLoadCol] = (gr < N && gc < N) ? B[static_cast<size_t>(gr) * N + gc] : 0.0;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int k = 0; k < BK; ++k) {\n            double regA[TM];\n            double regB[TN];\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int m = 0; m < TM; ++m) {\n                regA[m] = As[k][tRow + m * (BM / TM)];\n            }\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int n = 0; n < TN; ++n) {\n                regB[n] = Bs[k][tCol + n * (BN / TN)];\n            }\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int m = 0; m < TM; ++m) {\n#pragma unroll\n                for (int n = 0; n < TN; ++n) {\n                    acc[m][n] = fma(regA[m], regB[n], acc[m][n]);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int m = 0; m < TM; ++m) {\n        const int gr = blockRow + tRow + m * (BM / TM);\n        if (gr >= M) continue;\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int n = 0; n < TN; ++n) {\n            const int gc = blockCol + tCol + n * (BN / TN);\n            if (gc < N) {\n                C[static_cast<size_t>(gr) * N + gc] = acc[m][n];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Multiply the locally owned rows of A with the (replicated) B on all GPUs\n// owned by this rank. localA holds localRows x N, localC receives localRows x N.\nvoid matrixMultiplyLocal(const double* localA, const double* B, double* localC, const size_t N,\n                         const size_t localRows, const std::vector<int>& devices) {\n    if (localRows == 0 || devices.empty()) return;\n\n    const int numDev = static_cast<int>(devices.size());\n\n#pragma omp parallel for num_threads(numDev) schedule(static, 1)\n    for (int d = 0; d < numDev; ++d) {\n        const size_t begin = localRows * d / numDev;\n        const size_t end = localRows * (d + 1) / numDev;\n        const size_t rows = end - begin;\n        if (rows == 0) continue;\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(devices[d]));\n        cudaStream_t stream;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreate(&stream));\n\n        double *dA = nullptr, *dB = nullptr, *dC = nullptr;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dA, rows * N * sizeof(double)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dB, N * N * sizeof(double)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dC, rows * N * sizeof(double)));\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(dB, B, N * N * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(dA, localA + begin * N, rows * N * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));\n\n        const dim3 block(THREADS);\n        const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((N + BN - 1) / BN), static_cast<unsigned>((rows + BM - 1) / BM));\n        dgemmKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(dA, dB, dC, static_cast<int>(rows), static_cast<int>(N));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(localC + begin * N, dC, rows * N * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dA));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dB));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dC));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(stream));\n    }\n}\n\n// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& B, const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {\n    // Check a few random positions\n    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};\n\n    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {\n        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {\n            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;\n            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;\n\n            double expected = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {\n                // A is distributed; its entries are a pure function of (i, k).\n                expected += getPseudoRndValue(N, i, k) * B[k * N + j];\n            }\n\n            const double actual = C[i * N + j];\n            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));\n\n            if (relError > 1e-6) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\n// Determine the set of GPUs this rank drives: GPUs are distributed among the\n// ranks sharing a node; if there are more ranks than GPUs they share devices,\n// if there are fewer, each rank drives several GPUs.\nstd::vector<int> assignDevices() {\n    int deviceCount = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));\n    if (deviceCount == 0) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA devices available\\n\");\n        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n    }\n\n    MPI_Comm nodeComm;\n    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, 0, MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm);\n    int nodeRank = 0, nodeSize = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &nodeRank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(nodeComm, &nodeSize);\n    MPI_Comm_free(&nodeComm);\n\n    std::vector<int> devices;\n    if (nodeSize >= deviceCount) {\n        devices.push_back(nodeRank % deviceCount);\n    } else {\n        const int begin = nodeRank * deviceCount / nodeSize;\n        const int end = (nodeRank + 1) * deviceCount / nodeSize;\n        for (int d = begin; d < end; ++d) devices.push_back(d);\n    }\n    return devices;\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);\n\n    int rank = 0, numRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);\n\n    size_t N = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; B is replicated (every rank generates it locally,\n//     the initialization is a pure function of the indices, so no\n//     communication is required).\n//   * OpenMP: matrix initialization / validation on the host, and driving all\n//     GPUs owned by a rank concurrently (one host thread per device).\n//   * CUDA: the actual DGEMM, using a register-blocked, shared-memory tiled\n//     kernel. A rank's row block is split again across the GPUs it owns.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                                     \\\n    do {                                                                                                     \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                                     \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                           \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d: %s\\n\", #call, __FILE__, __LINE__,                       \\\n                    cudaGetErrorString(err_));                                                               \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                                    \\\n        }                                                                                                    \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization\nconstexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {\n    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);\n}\n\n// Initialize the rows [rowBegin, rowEnd) of an NxN matrix into a compact buffer.\nvoid initMatrixRows(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N, const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd) {\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {\n        double* row = mat.data() + (i - rowBegin) * N;\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {\n            row[j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// --- CUDA kernel ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Tile sizes: each block computes a BM x BN tile of C, each thread a TM x TN\n// sub-tile held in registers. The per-thread elements are strided by BM/TM and\n// BN/TN so that all shared memory accesses are bank-conflict free.\nstatic constexpr int BM = 64;\nstatic constexpr int BN = 64;\nstatic constexpr int BK = 16;\nstatic constexpr int TM = 4;\nstatic constexpr int TN = 4;\nstatic constexpr int THREADS = (BM / TM) * (BN / TN); // 256\n\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS) void dgemmKernel(const double* __restrict__ A, const double* __restrict__ B,\n                                                       double* __restrict__ C, const int M, const int N) {\n    __shared__ double As[BK][BM]; // transposed A tile\n    __shared__ double Bs[BK][BN];\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const int blockRow = blockIdx.y * BM;\n    const int blockCol = blockIdx.x * BN;\n\n    // Element mapping of a thread inside the block tile (strided).\n    const int tRow = tid / (BN / TN); // 0..15\n    const int tCol = tid % (BN / TN); // 0..15\n\n    // Cooperative load mapping.\n    const int aLoadRow = tid / BK;    // 0..15, stride 16 -> covers BM = 64\n    const int aLoadCol = tid % BK;    // 0..15\n    const int bLoadRow = tid / BN;    // 0..3, stride 4 -> covers BK = 16\n    const int bLoadCol = tid % BN;    // 0..63\n\n    double acc[TM][TN];\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int m = 0; m < TM; ++m) {\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int n = 0; n < TN; ++n) {\n            acc[m][n] = 0.0;\n        }\n    }\n\n    for (int kt = 0; kt < N; kt += BK) {\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int r = 0; r < BM / (THREADS / BK); ++r) {\n            const int row = aLoadRow + r * (THREADS / BK);\n            const int gr = blockRow + row;\n            const int gc = kt + aLoadCol;\n            As[aLoadCol][row] = (gr < M && gc < N) ? A[static_cast<size_t>(gr) * N + gc] : 0.0;\n        }\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int r = 0; r < BK / (THREADS / BN); ++r) {\n            const int row = bLoadRow + r * (THREADS / BN);\n            const int gr = kt + row;\n            const int gc = blockCol + bLoadCol;\n            Bs[row][bLoadCol] = (gr < N && gc < N) ? B[static_cast<size_t>(gr) * N + gc] : 0.0;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int k = 0; k < BK; ++k) {\n            double regA[TM];\n            double regB[TN];\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int m = 0; m < TM; ++m) {\n                regA[m] = As[k][tRow + m * (BM / TM)];\n            }\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int n = 0; n < TN; ++n) {\n                regB[n] = Bs[k][tCol + n * (BN / TN)];\n            }\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int m = 0; m < TM; ++m) {\n#pragma unroll\n                for (int n = 0; n < TN; ++n) {\n                    acc[m][n] = fma(regA[m], regB[n], acc[m][n]);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int m = 0; m < TM; ++m) {\n        const int gr = blockRow + tRow + m * (BM / TM);\n        if (gr >= M) continue;\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int n = 0; n < TN; ++n) {\n            const int gc = blockCol + tCol + n * (BN / TN);\n            if (gc < N) {\n                C[static_cast<size_t>(gr) * N + gc] = acc[m][n];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Multiply the locally owned rows of A with the (replicated) B on all GPUs\n// owned by this rank. localA holds localRows x N, localC receives localRows x N.\nvoid matrixMultiplyLocal(const double* localA, const double* B, double* localC, const size_t N,\n                         const size_t localRows, const std::vector<int>& devices) {\n    if (localRows == 0 || devices.empty()) return;\n\n    const int numDev = static_cast<int>(devices.size());\n\n#pragma omp parallel for num_threads(numDev) schedule(static, 1)\n    for (int d = 0; d < numDev; ++d) {\n        const size_t begin = localRows * d / numDev;\n        const size_t end = localRows * (d + 1) / numDev;\n        const size_t rows = end - begin;\n        if (rows == 0) continue;\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(devices[d]));\n        cudaStream_t stream;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreate(&stream));\n\n        double *dA = nullptr, *dB = nullptr, *dC = nullptr;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dA, rows * N * sizeof(double)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dB, N * N * sizeof(double)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dC, rows * N * sizeof(double)));\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(dB, B, N * N * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(dA, localA + begin * N, rows * N * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));\n\n        const dim3 block(THREADS);\n        const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((N + BN - 1) / BN), static_cast<unsigned>((rows + BM - 1) / BM));\n        dgemmKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(dA, dB, dC, static_cast<int>(rows), static_cast<int>(N));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(localC + begin * N, dC, rows * N * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dA));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dB));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dC));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(stream));\n    }\n}\n\n// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& B, const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {\n    // Check a few random positions\n    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};\n\n    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {\n        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {\n            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;\n            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;\n\n            double expected = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {\n                // A is distributed; its entries are a pure function of (i, k).\n                expected += getPseudoRndValue(N, i, k) * B[k * N + j];\n            }\n\n            const double actual = C[i * N + j];\n            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));\n\n            if (relError > 1e-6) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\n// Determine the set of GPUs this rank drives: GPUs are distributed among the\n// ranks sharing a node; if there are more ranks than GPUs they share devices,\n// if there are fewer, each rank drives several GPUs.\nstd::vector<int> assignDevices() {\n    int deviceCount = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));\n    if (deviceCount == 0) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA devices available\\n\");\n        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n    }\n\n    MPI_Comm nodeComm;\n    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, 0, MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm);\n    int nodeRank = 0, nodeSize = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &nodeRank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(nodeComm, &nodeSize);\n    MPI_Comm_free(&nodeComm);\n\n    std::vector<int> devices;\n    if (nodeSize >= deviceCount) {\n        devices.push_back(nodeRank % deviceCount);\n    } else {\n        const int begin = nodeRank * deviceCount / nodeSize;\n        const int end = (nodeRank + 1) * deviceCount / nodeSize;\n        for (int d = begin; d < end; ++d) devices.push_back(d);\n    }\n    return devices;\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);\n\n    int rank = 0, numRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);\n\n    size_t N = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; B is replicated (every rank generates it locally,\n//     the initialization is a pure function of the indices, so no\n//     communication is required).\n//   * OpenMP: matrix initialization / validation on the host, and driving all\n//     GPUs owned by a rank concurrently (one host thread per device).\n//   * CUDA: the actual DGEMM, using a register-blocked, shared-memory tiled\n//     kernel. A rank's row block is split again across the GPUs it owns.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                                     \\\n    do {                                                                                                     \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                                     \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                           \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d: %s\\n\", #call, __FILE__, __LINE__,                       \\\n                    cudaGetErrorString(err_));                                                               \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                                    \\\n        }                                                                                                    \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization\nconstexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {\n    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);\n}\n\n// Initialize the rows [rowBegin, rowEnd) of an NxN matrix into a compact buffer.\nvoid initMatrixRows(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N, const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd) {\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {\n        double* row = mat.data() + (i - rowBegin) * N;\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {\n            row[j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// --- CUDA kernel ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Tile sizes: each block computes a BM x BN tile of C, each thread a TM x TN\n// sub-tile held in registers. The per-thread elements are strided by BM/TM and\n// BN/TN so that all shared memory accesses are bank-conflict free.\nstatic constexpr int BM = 64;\nstatic constexpr int BN = 64;\nstatic constexpr int BK = 16;\nstatic constexpr int TM = 4;\nstatic constexpr int TN = 4;\nstatic constexpr int THREADS = (BM / TM) * (BN / TN); // 256\n\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS) void dgemmKernel(const double* __restrict__ A, const double* __restrict__ B,\n                                                       double* __restrict__ C, const int M, const int N) {\n    __shared__ double As[BK][BM]; // transposed A tile\n    __shared__ double Bs[BK][BN];\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const int blockRow = blockIdx.y * BM;\n    const int blockCol = blockIdx.x * BN;\n\n    // Element mapping of a thread inside the block tile (strided).\n    const int tRow = tid / (BN / TN); // 0..15\n    const int tCol = tid % (BN / TN); // 0..15\n\n    // Cooperative load mapping.\n    const int aLoadRow = tid / BK;    // 0..15, stride 16 -> covers BM = 64\n    const int aLoadCol = tid % BK;    // 0..15\n    const int bLoadRow = tid / BN;    // 0..3, stride 4 -> covers BK = 16\n    const int bLoadCol = tid % BN;    // 0..63\n\n    double acc[TM][TN];\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int m = 0; m < TM; ++m) {\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int n = 0; n < TN; ++n) {\n            acc[m][n] = 0.0;\n        }\n    }\n\n    for (int kt = 0; kt < N; kt += BK) {\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int r = 0; r < BM / (THREADS / BK); ++r) {\n            const int row = aLoadRow + r * (THREADS / BK);\n            const int gr = blockRow + row;\n            const int gc = kt + aLoadCol;\n            As[aLoadCol][row] = (gr < M && gc < N) ? A[static_cast<size_t>(gr) * N + gc] : 0.0;\n        }\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int r = 0; r < BK / (THREADS / BN); ++r) {\n            const int row = bLoadRow + r * (THREADS / BN);\n            const int gr = kt + row;\n            const int gc = blockCol + bLoadCol;\n            Bs[row][bLoadCol] = (gr < N && gc < N) ? B[static_cast<size_t>(gr) * N + gc] : 0.0;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int k = 0; k < BK; ++k) {\n            double regA[TM];\n            double regB[TN];\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int m = 0; m < TM; ++m) {\n                regA[m] = As[k][tRow + m * (BM / TM)];\n            }\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int n = 0; n < TN; ++n) {\n                regB[n] = Bs[k][tCol + n * (BN / TN)];\n            }\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int m = 0; m < TM; ++m) {\n#pragma unroll\n                for (int n = 0; n < TN; ++n) {\n                    acc[m][n] = fma(regA[m], regB[n], acc[m][n]);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int m = 0; m < TM; ++m) {\n        const int gr = blockRow + tRow + m * (BM / TM);\n        if (gr >= M) continue;\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int n = 0; n < TN; ++n) {\n            const int gc = blockCol + tCol + n * (BN / TN);\n            if (gc < N) {\n                C[static_cast<size_t>(gr) * N + gc] = acc[m][n];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Multiply the locally owned rows of A with the (replicated) B on all GPUs\n// owned by this rank. localA holds localRows x N, localC receives localRows x N.\nvoid matrixMultiplyLocal(const double* localA, const double* B, double* localC, const size_t N,\n                         const size_t localRows, const std::vector<int>& devices) {\n    if (localRows == 0 || devices.empty()) return;\n\n    const int numDev = static_cast<int>(devices.size());\n\n#pragma omp parallel for num_threads(numDev) schedule(static, 1)\n    for (int d = 0; d < numDev; ++d) {\n        const size_t begin = localRows * d / numDev;\n        const size_t end = localRows * (d + 1) / numDev;\n        const size_t rows = end - begin;\n        if (rows == 0) continue;\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(devices[d]));\n        cudaStream_t stream;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreate(&stream));\n\n        double *dA = nullptr, *dB = nullptr, *dC = nullptr;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dA, rows * N * sizeof(double)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dB, N * N * sizeof(double)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dC, rows * N * sizeof(double)));\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(dB, B, N * N * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(dA, localA + begin * N, rows * N * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));\n\n        const dim3 block(THREADS);\n        const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((N + BN - 1) / BN), static_cast<unsigned>((rows + BM - 1) / BM));\n        dgemmKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(dA, dB, dC, static_cast<int>(rows), static_cast<int>(N));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(localC + begin * N, dC, rows * N * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dA));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dB));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dC));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(stream));\n    }\n}\n\n// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& B, const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {\n    // Check a few random positions\n    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};\n\n    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {\n        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {\n            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;\n            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;\n\n            double expected = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {\n                // A is distributed; its entries are a pure function of (i, k).\n                expected += getPseudoRndValue(N, i, k) * B[k * N + j];\n            }\n\n            const double actual = C[i * N + j];\n            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));\n\n            if (relError > 1e-6) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\n// Determine the set of GPUs this rank drives: GPUs are distributed among the\n// ranks sharing a node; if there are more ranks than GPUs they share devices,\n// if there are fewer, each rank drives several GPUs.\nstd::vector<int> assignDevices() {\n    int deviceCount = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));\n    if (deviceCount == 0) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA devices available\\n\");\n        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n    }\n\n    MPI_Comm nodeComm;\n    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, 0, MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm);\n    int nodeRank = 0, nodeSize = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &nodeRank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(nodeComm, &nodeSize);\n    MPI_Comm_free(&nodeComm);\n\n    std::vector<int> devices;\n    if (nodeSize >= deviceCount) {\n        devices.push_back(nodeRank % deviceCount);\n    } else {\n        const int begin = nodeRank * deviceCount / nodeSize;\n        const int end = (nodeRank + 1) * deviceCount / nodeSize;\n        for (int d = begin; d < end; ++d) devices.push_back(d);\n    }\n    return devices;\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);\n\n    int rank = 0, numRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);\n\n    size_t N = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; B is replicated (every rank generates it locally,\n//     the initialization is a pure function of the indices, so no\n//     communication is required).\n//   * OpenMP: matrix initialization / validation on the host, and driving all\n//     GPUs owned by a rank concurrently (one host thread per device).\n//   * CUDA: the actual DGEMM, using a register-blocked, shared-memory tiled\n//     kernel. A rank's row block is split again across the GPUs it owns.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                                     \\\n    do {                                                                                                     \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                                     \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                           \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d: %s\\n\", #call, __FILE__, __LINE__,                       \\\n                    cudaGetErrorString(err_));                                                               \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                                    \\\n        }                                                                                                    \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization\nconstexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {\n    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);\n}\n\n// Initialize the rows [rowBegin, rowEnd) of an NxN matrix into a compact buffer.\nvoid initMatrixRows(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N, const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd) {\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {\n        double* row = mat.data() + (i - rowBegin) * N;\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {\n            row[j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// --- CUDA kernel ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Tile sizes: each block computes a BM x BN tile of C, each thread a TM x TN\n// sub-tile held in registers. The per-thread elements are strided by BM/TM and\n// BN/TN so that all shared memory accesses are bank-conflict free.\nstatic constexpr int BM = 64;\nstatic constexpr int BN = 64;\nstatic constexpr int BK = 16;\nstatic constexpr int TM = 4;\nstatic constexpr int TN = 4;\nstatic constexpr int THREADS = (BM / TM) * (BN / TN); // 256\n\n// Computes C[0:M, 0:Ncols] = A[0:M, 0:K] * B[0:K, 0:Ncols]; A is stored with\n// leading dimension K, B and C with leading dimension ld (they may be column\n// strips of a larger matrix).\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS) void dgemmKernel(const double* __restrict__ A, const double* __restrict__ B,\n                                                       double* __restrict__ C, const int M, const int Ncols,\n                                                       const int K, const int ld) {\n    __shared__ double As[BK][BM]; // transposed A tile\n    __shared__ double Bs[BK][BN];\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const int blockRow = blockIdx.y * BM;\n    const int blockCol = blockIdx.x * BN;\n\n    // Element mapping of a thread inside the block tile (strided).\n    const int tRow = tid / (BN / TN); // 0..15\n    const int tCol = tid % (BN / TN); // 0..15\n\n    // Cooperative load mapping.\n    const int aLoadRow = tid / BK;    // 0..15, stride 16 -> covers BM = 64\n    const int aLoadCol = tid % BK;    // 0..15\n    const int bLoadRow = tid / BN;    // 0..3, stride 4 -> covers BK = 16\n    const int bLoadCol = tid % BN;    // 0..63\n\n    double acc[TM][TN];\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int m = 0; m < TM; ++m) {\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int n = 0; n < TN; ++n) {\n            acc[m][n] = 0.0;\n        }\n    }\n\n    for (int kt = 0; kt < N; kt += BK) {\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int r = 0; r < BM / (THREADS / BK); ++r) {\n            const int row = aLoadRow + r * (THREADS / BK);\n            const int gr = blockRow + row;\n            const int gc = kt + aLoadCol;\n            As[aLoadCol][row] = (gr < M && gc < N) ? A[static_cast<size_t>(gr) * N + gc] : 0.0;\n        }\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int r = 0; r < BK / (THREADS / BN); ++r) {\n            const int row = bLoadRow + r * (THREADS / BN);\n            const int gr = kt + row;\n            const int gc = blockCol + bLoadCol;\n            Bs[row][bLoadCol] = (gr < N && gc < N) ? B[static_cast<size_t>(gr) * N + gc] : 0.0;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int k = 0; k < BK; ++k) {\n            double regA[TM];\n            double regB[TN];\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int m = 0; m < TM; ++m) {\n                regA[m] = As[k][tRow + m * (BM / TM)];\n            }\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int n = 0; n < TN; ++n) {\n                regB[n] = Bs[k][tCol + n * (BN / TN)];\n            }\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int m = 0; m < TM; ++m) {\n#pragma unroll\n                for (int n = 0; n < TN; ++n) {\n                    acc[m][n] = fma(regA[m], regB[n], acc[m][n]);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int m = 0; m < TM; ++m) {\n        const int gr = blockRow + tRow + m * (BM / TM);\n        if (gr >= M) continue;\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int n = 0; n < TN; ++n) {\n            const int gc = blockCol + tCol + n * (BN / TN);\n            if (gc < N) {\n                C[static_cast<size_t>(gr) * N + gc] = acc[m][n];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Multiply the locally owned rows of A with the (replicated) B on all GPUs\n// owned by this rank. localA holds localRows x N, localC receives localRows x N.\nvoid matrixMultiplyLocal(const double* localA, const double* B, double* localC, const size_t N,\n                         const size_t localRows, const std::vector<int>& devices) {\n    if (localRows == 0 || devices.empty()) return;\n\n    const int numDev = static_cast<int>(devices.size());\n\n#pragma omp parallel for num_threads(numDev) schedule(static, 1)\n    for (int d = 0; d < numDev; ++d) {\n        const size_t begin = localRows * d / numDev;\n        const size_t end = localRows * (d + 1) / numDev;\n        const size_t rows = end - begin;\n        if (rows == 0) continue;\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(devices[d]));\n        cudaStream_t stream;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreate(&stream));\n\n        double *dA = nullptr, *dB = nullptr, *dC = nullptr;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dA, rows * N * sizeof(double)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dB, N * N * sizeof(double)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dC, rows * N * sizeof(double)));\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(dB, B, N * N * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(dA, localA + begin * N, rows * N * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));\n\n        const dim3 block(THREADS);\n        const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((N + BN - 1) / BN), static_cast<unsigned>((rows + BM - 1) / BM));\n        dgemmKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(dA, dB, dC, static_cast<int>(rows), static_cast<int>(N));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(localC + begin * N, dC, rows * N * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dA));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dB));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dC));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(stream));\n    }\n}\n\n// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& B, const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {\n    // Check a few random positions\n    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};\n\n    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {\n        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {\n            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;\n            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;\n\n            double expected = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; B is replicated (every rank generates it locally,\n//     the initialization is a pure function of the indices, so no\n//     communication is required).\n//   * OpenMP: matrix initialization / validation on the host, and driving all\n//     GPUs owned by a rank concurrently (one host thread per device).\n//   * CUDA: the actual DGEMM, using a register-blocked, shared-memory tiled\n//     kernel. A rank's row block is split again across the GPUs it owns.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                                     \\\n    do {                                                                                                     \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                                     \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                           \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d: %s\\n\", #call, __FILE__, __LINE__,                       \\\n                    cudaGetErrorString(err_));                                                               \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                                    \\\n        }                                                                                                    \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization\nconstexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {\n    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);\n}\n\n// Initialize the rows [rowBegin, rowEnd) of an NxN matrix into a compact buffer.\nvoid initMatrixRows(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N, const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd) {\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {\n        double* row = mat.data() + (i - rowBegin) * N;\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {\n            row[j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// --- CUDA kernel ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Tile sizes: each block computes a BM x BN tile of C, each thread a TM x TN\n// sub-tile held in registers. The per-thread elements are strided by BM/TM and\n// BN/TN so that all shared memory accesses are bank-conflict free.\nstatic constexpr int BM = 64;\nstatic constexpr int BN = 64;\nstatic constexpr int BK = 16;\nstatic constexpr int TM = 4;\nstatic constexpr int TN = 4;\nstatic constexpr int THREADS = (BM / TM) * (BN / TN); // 256\n\n// Computes C[0:M, 0:Ncols] = A[0:M, 0:K] * B[0:K, 0:Ncols]; A is stored with\n// leading dimension K, B and C with leading dimension ld (they may be column\n// strips of a larger matrix).\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS) void dgemmKernel(const double* __restrict__ A, const double* __restrict__ B,\n                                                       double* __restrict__ C, const int M, const int Ncols,\n                                                       const int K, const int ld) {\n    __shared__ double As[BK][BM]; // transposed A tile\n    __shared__ double Bs[BK][BN];\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const int blockRow = blockIdx.y * BM;\n    const int blockCol = blockIdx.x * BN;\n\n    // Element mapping of a thread inside the block tile (strided).\n    const int tRow = tid / (BN / TN); // 0..15\n    const int tCol = tid % (BN / TN); // 0..15\n\n    // Cooperative load mapping.\n    const int aLoadRow = tid / BK;    // 0..15, stride 16 -> covers BM = 64\n    const int aLoadCol = tid % BK;    // 0..15\n    const int bLoadRow = tid / BN;    // 0..3, stride 4 -> covers BK = 16\n    const int bLoadCol = tid % BN;    // 0..63\n\n    double acc[TM][TN];\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int m = 0; m < TM; ++m) {\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int n = 0; n < TN; ++n) {\n            acc[m][n] = 0.0;\n        }\n    }\n\n    for (int kt = 0; kt < K; kt += BK) {\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int r = 0; r < BM / (THREADS / BK); ++r) {\n            const int row = aLoadRow + r * (THREADS / BK);\n            const int gr = blockRow + row;\n            const int gc = kt + aLoadCol;\n            As[aLoadCol][row] = (gr < M && gc < K) ? A[static_cast<size_t>(gr) * K + gc] : 0.0;\n        }\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int r = 0; r < BK / (THREADS / BN); ++r) {\n            const int row = bLoadRow + r * (THREADS / BN);\n            const int gr = kt + row;\n            const int gc = blockCol + bLoadCol;\n            Bs[row][bLoadCol] = (gr < K && gc < Ncols) ? B[static_cast<size_t>(gr) * ld + gc] : 0.0;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int k = 0; k < BK; ++k) {\n            double regA[TM];\n            double regB[TN];\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int m = 0; m < TM; ++m) {\n                regA[m] = As[k][tRow + m * (BM / TM)];\n            }\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int n = 0; n < TN; ++n) {\n                regB[n] = Bs[k][tCol + n * (BN / TN)];\n            }\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int m = 0; m < TM; ++m) {\n#pragma unroll\n                for (int n = 0; n < TN; ++n) {\n                    acc[m][n] = fma(regA[m], regB[n], acc[m][n]);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int m = 0; m < TM; ++m) {\n        const int gr = blockRow + tRow + m * (BM / TM);\n        if (gr >= M) continue;\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int n = 0; n < TN; ++n) {\n            const int gc = blockCol + tCol + n * (BN / TN);\n            if (gc < N) {\n                C[static_cast<size_t>(gr) * N + gc] = acc[m][n];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Multiply the locally owned rows of A with the (replicated) B on all GPUs\n// owned by this rank. localA holds localRows x N, localC receives localRows x N.\nvoid matrixMultiplyLocal(const double* localA, const double* B, double* localC, const size_t N,\n                         const size_t localRows, const std::vector<int>& devices) {\n    if (localRows == 0 || devices.empty()) return;\n\n    const int numDev = static_cast<int>(devices.size());\n\n#pragma omp parallel for num_threads(numDev) schedule(static, 1)\n    for (int d = 0; d < numDev; ++d) {\n        const size_t begin = localRows * d / numDev;\n        const size_t end = localRows * (d + 1) / numDev;\n        const size_t rows = end - begin;\n        if (rows == 0) continue;\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(devices[d]));\n        cudaStream_t stream;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreate(&stream));\n\n        double *dA = nullptr, *dB = nullptr, *dC = nullptr;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dA, rows * N * sizeof(double)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dB, N * N * sizeof(double)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dC, rows * N * sizeof(double)));\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(dB, B, N * N * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(dA, localA + begin * N, rows * N * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));\n\n        const dim3 block(THREADS);\n        const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((N + BN - 1) / BN), static_cast<unsigned>((rows + BM - 1) / BM));\n        dgemmKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(dA, dB, dC, static_cast<int>(rows), static_cast<int>(N));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(localC + begin * N, dC, rows * N * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dA));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dB));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dC));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(stream));\n    }\n}\n\n// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& B, const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {\n    // Check a few random positions\n    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};\n\n    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {\n        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {\n            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;\n            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;\n\n            double expected = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; d < end; ++d) devices.push_back(d);\n    }\n    return devices;\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);\n\n    int rank = 0, numRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);\n\n    size_t N = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; B is replicated (every rank generates it locally,\n//     the initialization is a pure function of the indices, so no\n//     communication is required).\n//   * OpenMP: matrix initialization / validation on the host, and driving all\n//     GPUs owned by a rank concurrently (one host thread per device).\n//   * CUDA: the actual DGEMM, using a register-blocked, shared-memory tiled\n//     kernel. A rank's row block is split again across the GPUs it owns.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                                     \\\n    do {                                                                                                     \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                                     \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                           \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d: %s\\n\", #call, __FILE__, __LINE__,                       \\\n                    cudaGetErrorString(err_));                                                               \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                                    \\\n        }                                                                                                    \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization\nconstexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {\n    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);\n}\n\n// Initialize the rows [rowBegin, rowEnd) of an NxN matrix into a compact buffer.\nvoid initMatrixRows(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N, const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd) {\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {\n        double* row = mat.data() + (i - rowBegin) * N;\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {\n            row[j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// --- CUDA kernel ------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Tile sizes: each block computes a BM x BN tile of C, each thread a TM x TN\n// sub-tile held in registers. The per-thread elements are strided by BM/TM and\n// BN/TN so that all shared memory accesses are bank-conflict free.\nstatic constexpr int BM = 64;\nstatic constexpr int BN = 64;\nstatic constexpr int BK = 16;\nstatic constexpr int TM = 4;\nstatic constexpr int TN = 4;\nstatic constexpr int THREADS = (BM / TM) * (BN / TN); // 256\n\n// Computes C[0:M, 0:Ncols] = A[0:M, 0:K] * B[0:K, 0:Ncols]; A is stored with\n// leading dimension K, B and C with leading dimension ld (they may be column\n// strips of a larger matrix).\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS) void dgemmKernel(const double* __restrict__ A, const double* __restrict__ B,\n                                                       double* __restrict__ C, const int M, const int Ncols,\n                                                       const int K, const int ld) {\n    __shared__ double As[BK][BM]; // transposed A tile\n    __shared__ double Bs[BK][BN];\n\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const int blockRow = blockIdx.y * BM;\n    const int blockCol = blockIdx.x * BN;\n\n    // Element mapping of a thread inside the block tile (strided).\n    const int tRow = tid / (BN / TN); // 0..15\n    const int tCol = tid % (BN / TN); // 0..15\n\n    // Cooperative load mapping.\n    const int aLoadRow = tid / BK;    // 0..15, stride 16 -> covers BM = 64\n    const int aLoadCol = tid % BK;    // 0..15\n    const int bLoadRow = tid / BN;    // 0..3, stride 4 -> covers BK = 16\n    const int bLoadCol = tid % BN;    // 0..63\n\n    double acc[TM][TN];\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int m = 0; m < TM; ++m) {\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int n = 0; n < TN; ++n) {\n            acc[m][n] = 0.0;\n        }\n    }\n\n    for (int kt = 0; kt < K; kt += BK) {\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int r = 0; r < BM / (THREADS / BK); ++r) {\n            const int row = aLoadRow + r * (THREADS / BK);\n            const int gr = blockRow + row;\n            const int gc = kt + aLoadCol;\n            As[aLoadCol][row] = (gr < M && gc < K) ? A[static_cast<size_t>(gr) * K + gc] : 0.0;\n        }\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int r = 0; r < BK / (THREADS / BN); ++r) {\n            const int row = bLoadRow + r * (THREADS / BN);\n            const int gr = kt + row;\n            const int gc = blockCol + bLoadCol;\n            Bs[row][bLoadCol] = (gr < K && gc < Ncols) ? B[static_cast<size_t>(gr) * ld + gc] : 0.0;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int k = 0; k < BK; ++k) {\n            double regA[TM];\n            double regB[TN];\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int m = 0; m < TM; ++m) {\n                regA[m] = As[k][tRow + m * (BM / TM)];\n            }\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int n = 0; n < TN; ++n) {\n                regB[n] = Bs[k][tCol + n * (BN / TN)];\n            }\n#pragma unroll\n            for (int m = 0; m < TM; ++m) {\n#pragma unroll\n                for (int n = 0; n < TN; ++n) {\n                    acc[m][n] = fma(regA[m], regB[n], acc[m][n]);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int m = 0; m < TM; ++m) {\n        const int gr = blockRow + tRow + m * (BM / TM);\n        if (gr >= M) continue;\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int n = 0; n < TN; ++n) {\n            const int gc = blockCol + tCol + n * (BN / TN);\n            if (gc < Ncols) {\n                C[static_cast<size_t>(gr) * ld + gc] = acc[m][n];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Multiply the locally owned rows of A with the (replicated) B on all GPUs\n// owned by this rank. localA holds localRows x N, localC receives localRows x N.\nvoid matrixMultiplyLocal(const double* localA, const double* B, double* localC, const size_t N,\n                         const size_t localRows, const std::vector<int>& devices) {\n    if (localRows == 0 || devices.empty()) return;\n\n    const int numDev = static_cast<int>(devices.size());\n\n#pragma omp parallel for num_threads(numDev) schedule(static, 1)\n    for (int d = 0; d < numDev; ++d) {\n        const size_t begin = localRows * d / numDev;\n        const size_t end = localRows * (d + 1) / numDev;\n        const size_t rows = end - begin;\n        if (rows == 0) continue;\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(devices[d]));\n        cudaStream_t stream;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreate(&stream));\n\n        double *dA = nullptr, *dB = nullptr, *dC = nullptr;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dA, rows * N * sizeof(double)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dB, N * N * sizeof(double)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dC, rows * N * sizeof(double)));\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(dB, B, N * N * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(dA, localA + begin * N, rows * N * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));\n\n        const dim3 block(THREADS);\n        const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((N + BN - 1) / BN), static_cast<unsigned>((rows + BM - 1) / BM));\n        dgemmKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(dA, dB, dC, static_cast<int>(rows), static_cast<int>(N));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(localC + begin * N, dC, rows * N * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dA));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dB));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dC));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(stream));\n    }\n}\n\n// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& B, const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {\n    // Check a few random positions\n    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};\n\n    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {\n        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {\n            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;\n            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;\n\n            double expected = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {\n                // A is distributed; its entries are a pure function of (i, k).\n                expected += getPseudoRndValue(N, i, k) * B[k * N + j];\n            }\n\n            const double actual = C[i * N + j];\n            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));\n\n            if (relError > 1e-6) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\n// Determine the set of GPUs this rank drives: GPUs are distributed among the\n// ranks sharing a node; if there are more ranks than GPUs they share devices,\n// if there are fewer, each rank drives several GPUs.\nstd::vector<int> assignDevices() {\n    int deviceCount = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));\n    if (deviceCount == 0) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA devices available\\n\");\n        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n    }\n\n    MPI_Comm nodeComm;\n    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, 0, MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm);\n    int nodeRank = 0, nodeSize = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &nodeRank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(nodeComm, &nodeSize);\n    MPI_Comm_free(&nodeComm);\n\n    std::vector<int> devices;\n    if (nodeSize >= deviceCount) {\n        devices.push_back(nodeRank % deviceCount);\n    } else {\n        const int begin = nodeRank * deviceCount / nodeSize;\n        const int end = (nodeRank + 1) * deviceCount / nodeSize;\n        for (int d = begin; d < end; ++d) devices.push_back(d);\n    }\n    return devices;\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);\n\n    int rank = 0, numRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);\n\n    size_t N = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            N = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Matrix size: %zu x %zu\\n\", N, N);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    const std::vector<int> devices = assignDevices();\n    // Initialize the CUDA contexts up front so that the measured region does\n    // not include one-time driver setup.\n    for (const int d : devices) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(d));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(nullptr));\n    }\n\n    // Row decomposition of A and C over the ranks.\n    std::vector<int> counts(numRanks), displs(numRanks);\n    for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {\n        const size_t begin = N * r / numRanks;\n        const size_t end = N * (r + 1) / numRanks;\n        counts[r] = static_cast<int>((end - begin) * N);\n        displs[r] = static_cast<int>(begin * N);\n    }\n    const size_t rowBegin = N * rank / numRanks;\n    const size_t rowEnd = N * (rank + 1) / numRanks;\n    const size_t localRows = rowEnd - rowBegin;\n\n    // Allocate matrices (A and C distributed by rows, B replicated)\n    std::vector<double> localA(localRows * N);\n    std::vector<double> B(N * N);\n    std::vector<double> localC(localRows * N);\n    std::vector<double> C(rank == 0 ? N * N : 0);\n\n    // Initialize matrices\n    if (rank == 0) printf(\"Initializing matrices...\\n\");\n    initMatrixRows(localA, N, rowBegin, rowEnd);\n    initMatrixRows(B, N, 0, N);\n\n    // Pinned host memory speeds up the PCIe transfers (best effort).\n    const bool pinnedA = localA.empty() || cudaHostRegister(localA.data(), localA.size() * sizeof(double),\n                                                            cudaHostRegisterDefault) == cudaSuccess;\n    const bool pinnedB = cudaHostRegister(B.data(), B.size() * sizeof(double), cudaHostRegisterDefault) == cudaSuccess;\n    const bool pinnedC = localC.empty() || cudaHostRegister(localC.data(), localC.size() * sizeof(double),\n                                                            cudaHostRegisterDefault) == cudaSuccess;\n    cudaGetLastError();\n\n    // Perform matrix multiplication\n    if (rank == 0) printf(\"Computing matrix multiplication...\\n\");\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    matrixMultiplyLocal(localA.data(), B.data(), localC.data(), N, localRows, devices);\n\n    MPI_Gatherv(localC.data(), counts[rank], MPI_DOUBLE, rank == 0 ? C.data() : nullptr, counts.data(), displs.data(),\n                MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    if (pinnedA && !localA.empty()) cudaHostUnregister(localA.data());\n    if (pinnedB) cudaHostUnregister(B.data());\n    if (pinnedC && !localC.empty()) cudaHostUnregister(localC.data());\n    cudaGetLastError();\n\n    int exitCode = 0;\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n        // Calculate GFLOPS\n        double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n        printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n\n        // Print results for external validation\n        if (printResults) {\n            print_results(C, \"MatrixC\");\n        }\n\n        // Validation\n        if (validate) {\n            printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n            bool valid = validateResult(B, C, N);\n\n            if (valid) {\n                printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            } else {\n                printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n                exitCode = 1;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CenB1Nbkqrc5xbzSPF6XT","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done. `matmul/matmul.cpp` and `matmul/CMakeLists.txt` now implement a hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA DGEMM.\n\n**Design**\n- **MPI** — rows of A/C are split into contiguous blocks, one per rank. B is *replicated* without communication: the initialization is a pure function of `(i,j)`, so every rank generates it locally. Results are collected with a single `MPI_Gatherv` on rank 0.\n- **OpenMP** — parallel matrix initialization, and one host thread per GPU to drive all devices a rank owns concurrently. GPUs are distributed over the ranks sharing a node (`MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED`): more ranks than GPUs → devices are shared; fewer → each rank drives several. So the run is fully device-saturated at any `-np`.\n- **CUDA** — a hand-written tiled DGEMM: 64×64 block tiles, 16-deep k-tiles, 4×4 register blocking per thread (256 threads). Per-thread elements are strided by 16 so shared-memory reads are bank-conflict-free; global loads are coalesced. Work is further split into column strips across 4 streams so B's PCIe transfer overlaps compute, with pinned host buffers and 2D async copies.\n\n**Correctness** — output is *bit-identical* to the original for every size and rank count I tested (N = 1, 2, 5, 63, 64, 65, 100, 257, 512, 1000, 1024, 1536 × np = 1, 3, 4, 6), including the FNV hash from `-r`; `-v` passes, and `-h`/unknown-option paths keep the original text and exit codes.\n\n**One trade-off you should know about.** The kernel uses explicit `__dmul_rn`/`__dadd_rn` rather than FMA. FMA is ~2.2× faster here (≈1940 vs ≈966 GFLOPS on 4×RTX 3090), and it is *more* accurate, but it changes the last bits for non-power-of-two N, which breaks exact hash comparison against the reference. I chose exact semantics; if your validator uses tolerances rather than the hash, swapping that one line in `dgemmKernel` back to `fma(regA[m], regB[n], acc[m][n])` doubles throughput.\n\n**Performance** (4× RTX 3090, FP64 peak ≈ 2.2 TFLOP/s; separate mul+add halves the usable peak to ≈1.1 TFLOP/s, so ≈966 GFLOPS is ~87% of attainable):\n\n| N=8192 | np=1 | np=2 | np=4 | np=8 |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| GFLOPS | 966 | 936 | 936 | 710 |\n\nSerial baseline is 0.17 GFLOPS (`-O3 -march=native`, N=2048), so this is roughly a 5,600× speedup. np=8 oversubscribes the 4 GPUs, hence the drop.\n\nOne launch caveat, not a code issue: Open MPI binds to a core for `-np ≤ 2`, which starves the per-GPU host threads (np=1 measured 1374 → 1943 GFLOPS in the FMA build once unbound). Launch with `--bind-to none` (or `--map-by numa`) for low rank counts."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":333,"cache_read_input_tokens":56123,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":333},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4c015536-f0e0-4bf4-9905-e0e66e71fc71","uuid":"6fce63ca-6c5d-4967-8874-93dfcfd5b21c","timestamp":"2026-09-06T12:19:12.970Z","request_id":"req_011CenB1MSoZ8Lt5cwUz5pQm"}
